Date:     2026-08-07
Current:  2.40%
90d low:  1.89%
Move:     +51bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year TIPS real yield rose to 2.40% as of 2026-08-07, up 0.51pp from its trailing-90-day low of 1.89%.

内容:
10 年期 TIPS 实际收益率截至 2026-08-07 升至 2.40%，较过去 90 天低点 1.89% 上升 0.51 个百分点。

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on long-duration AI-capex bets — a rise this size directly compresses the valuation of hyperscaler/neocloud names funding buildout with debt, and tightens the refinancing math the C7 credit-spread fuse also watches.

影响:
实际收益率是给 AI capex 这类长久期押注定价的贴现率，涨这么多会直接压低靠举债扩产的超大规模厂商/云厂商估值，也让 C7 信用利差那条引信盯着的再融资成本变紧。

What this means:
JPMorgan says the memory chip supply crunch — DRAM and NAND shortages — will persist for at least two more years.

内容:
JPMorgan 警告称，DRAM 和 NAND 的内存芯片供应紧张至少还要持续两年。

Why it matters:
A prolonged memory shortage keeps input costs high for every AI server builder and signals demand is structurally outrunning fab capacity, not just a temporary allocation squeeze. That supports the capex-is-rational bull case, but also means margin pressure on anyone buying memory rather than producing it.

影响:
内存短缺拖这么久，意味着所有做 AI 服务器的厂商成本都压不下来，也说明需求已经结构性地跑赢了晶圆产能，不只是临时性的供货紧张。这对「capex 花得值」的多头逻辑是支撑，但对靠采购内存（而非自己生产）的厂商是利润压力。

SpaceX's First Lockup Expired Last Week. Why Didn't the Stock Crash? - The Globe and Mail

<a href="https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMi4wFBVV95cUxPaURRTGQxTXRVLW1oNXNxNFQ4TGt2dER3V2xPVERkeXN2VmRGUTdqVWdrZDhCTDVKTGtZSC0zeERpSGg3N1I2amJYelZkak5xQ1YwUEtZR0IySE9DdmJhVkdWTEhYdTVEOUEzZ0t3QU5FY2JmRU5Na1RlTGk1N2tBbTlKOEVmaVFEVVNwRURuYTA4RmJMbVNWWTROdzRUVVBCVWd4UzF6YmV5MlRWWEx5cEhrN0YyLTlxSEd0NkFTaW54UDFHRS1neWZlRXZnUzJ5MnZwNGZIeWpYX1hiazREaHNNcw?oc=5" target="_blank">SpaceX's First Lockup Expired Last Week. What this means:
A news headline asks why SpaceX shares didn't drop after the company's first post-IPO lock-up expiration last week, implying insiders were newly free to sell but the stock held up.

内容:
一则新闻标题在问：SpaceX 上周迎来 IPO 后首次解禁，为什么股价没有下跌——按理内部人此时可以自由卖出了，但股价撑住了。

Why it matters:
A lock-up expiry that fails to trigger selling pressure either means insiders aren't dumping yet or demand is absorbing the new float — worth watching because C11's whole thesis is that staggered unlocks create mechanical, index-driven selling pressure independent of fundamentals; a no-crash outcome this early could mean the real stress is still ahead in later, larger tranches.

影响:
解禁后没砸盘，说明要么内部人还没开始抛，要么市场需求把新增流通盘吃掉了。这条值得盯，是因为 C11 整套逻辑就是分批解禁会带来和基本面无关的机械性抛压——这次没崩不代表没事，真正的压力可能在后面更大的解禁批次里。

What this means:
News coverage reports SpaceX shares surged 26% following the first lock-up expiration, despite the unlock theoretically adding sell-side supply to the market.

内容:
报道称，SpaceX 在首次解禁后股价反而上涨 26%，而解禁理论上会增加市场的抛售供给。

Why it matters:

What this means:
A news headline questions whether Apple faces a DRAM chip shortage ahead of the iPhone 18 Pro launch.

内容:
一则新闻质疑苹果在 iPhone 18 Pro 发布前是否面临 DRAM 芯片短缺。

Why it matters:
Rising DRAM tightness would squeeze margins for every AI-server buyer, not just Apple — memory is the bottleneck component behind GPU racks. A shortage severe enough to threaten a flagship phone launch signals capacity is stretched thinner than the 'plenty of supply' assumption in most AI-capex models.

影响:
DRAM 趋紧会压缩所有 AI 服务器采购方的利润空间，内存本来就是 GPU 机架背后的瓶颈部件。如果短缺严重到威胁旗舰手机发布，说明产能比多数 AI capex 模型假设的「供应充足」要紧张得多。

影响:
在估值刚创新高时出现这么大规模的二级市场解锁，是被动资金流和指数机制层面的信号——说明内部人正把账面浮盈变现，这种模式通常出现在私募 AI 基建估值见顶前后，往往是重新定价的前兆。
